# Agents · R · part 25

975 entries, researcher to result-synthesizer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **researcher**: Web/external research agent. Searches documentation, evaluates libraries, reads external sources. Use for non-codebase research. ([smantzavinos/engineering-agents](https://github.com/smantzavinos/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You source candidate library entries against a brief. You never bypass triple-verification. ([smiles70/thewiserlearner](https://github.com/smiles70/thewiserlearner/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigates a specific code/architecture/API question and returns a focused answer, keeping exploratory reads out of the main context before acting. ([smithadifd/skills](https://github.com/smithadifd/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only researcher for facts that must come from current primary sources. Use proactively when an answer depends on releases, versions, APIs, framework or model behavior, subscription facts, or vendor documentation that may have changed. ([snaplyze/claude-orchestration](https://github.com/snaplyze/claude-orchestration/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Autonomous external-evidence researcher — searches, evaluates, and synthesizes a focused research brief ([som1shi/pi-config](https://github.com/som1shi/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research the questions on the board. ([sonhal/herdr-teams](https://github.com/sonhal/herdr-teams/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research một concept cho stuff card mới - thu thập refs, đề xuất level/categories/links từ graph hiện có. Dùng cho bước 1 của content pipeline. PROACTIVELY dùng khi cần research concept từ backlog. ([sonkd/theunseen](https://github.com/sonkd/theunseen/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research an implementation question and return concise, sourced findings. ([sonwjnn/wincode](https://github.com/sonwjnn/wincode/blob/HEAD/packages/subagents/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research agent. Use for (a) finding and explaining things in the current repository, or (b) gathering information from external sources (docs, web). Returns a structured report with conclusions, evidence, references and a separate "not found" list. Asks clarifying questions first when the task is vague. ([Sotvorenniy/ai-market](https://github.com/Sotvorenniy/ai-market/blob/HEAD/plugins/research-tools/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigate only the assigned research question using local sources and current external evidence. Read repository instructions and honor the supplied scope, recency, and source constraints. If the question or success criteria are unclear, report the blocker and stop. ([soulwaxx/dotfiles](https://github.com/soulwaxx/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/shared/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use when a task depends on external docs or the web, before building, inside or outside /orchestrate. Sonnet read-only researcher. Verifies external facts before the planner splits work (library and API docs, version-specific behaviour, tool flags, protocols, firmware, known failure modes) and returns a short report where every claim has a source. ([soup426/claude-orchestrate](https://github.com/soup426/claude-orchestrate/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: あなたは調査の係です。ファイルは変更しません（編集の道具は使えません）。調べたいことに、根拠つきで答えます。 ([souten-yd/ControlDeck](https://github.com/souten-yd/ControlDeck/blob/HEAD/backend/app/integrations/opencode/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Looks things up on the web (docs, changelogs, prices, competitors) and returns a short sourced summary. Use for read-only research during kickoff or phase planning. ([speedsharmaai/phaseproof](https://github.com/speedsharmaai/phaseproof/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Answers a single factual question by searching the vault and the web, then returns a short digest with sources. Does not edit files and does not make decisions. Use whenever a question is findable rather than a judgment call, so the interview never asks a person for something the environment already knows. Dispatched by `grilling` for frontier questions and by `wayfinder` for research tickets. ([spencermarx/agentic-workspace-template](https://github.com/spencermarx/agentic-workspace-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Cited engineering research on one bounded question - tools, components, literature, regulations, datasheets. Use when a decision needs current external facts with sources; never for judgment calls or design decisions. ([srinator22/agent-kernel](https://github.com/srinator22/agent-kernel/blob/HEAD/docs/library/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Reads the requirements, investigates the existing codebase and external dependencies, and produces the research note and evidence the designer needs. Does not design. ([sson0-er/orchestraude](https://github.com/sson0-er/orchestraude/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Tests many variants of one ML direction in a bench competition repo, in its own git worktree, and returns a standard report table with CV and shadow scores against the baseline. Use with a filled brief from the research skill. ([STACK-42/bench](https://github.com/STACK-42/bench/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigate scoped questions using repository evidence and authoritative web sources; return sourced conclusions without mutations. ([stackingturtles/devproc](https://github.com/stackingturtles/devproc/blob/HEAD/harness/pi/agents/researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher**: Search and explore project files. Use for all codebase discovery tasks. ([StanislavMakhnytskyi/justhairs](https://github.com/StanislavMakhnytskyi/justhairs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Verifies external documentation, supported versions, and technical claims with cited sources. ([StefanNieuwenhuis/data-lakehouse](https://github.com/StefanNieuwenhuis/data-lakehouse/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/researcher.agent.md))
- **researcher**: Deep investigation — legal, compliance, market, technical feasibility. Gathers knowledge before decisions are made. ([StefanoChiodino/claude-split](https://github.com/StefanoChiodino/claude-split/blob/HEAD/split/agents/researcher.md))
- **Researcher**: You investigate codebases, documentation, and external sources to gather context. ([stefans71/Open-Pi-Harness-Advanced-V2](https://github.com/stefans71/Open-Pi-Harness-Advanced-V2/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Gathers evidence for the Discover stage from the web and the repo — never turning a finding into work. Forked in the background by the research skill; not invoked directly. ([stefnba/agentic-engineering-workflow](https://github.com/stefnba/agentic-engineering-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research on an open design question (methods, papers, model/API features). Use when a design choice needs evidence before code is written. Writes one note under docs/research/. ([stepanyanraffi15-cyber/questionnaire](https://github.com/stepanyanraffi15-cyber/questionnaire/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Delegate web and codebase research here to keep the caller's context small. Use when you need to find something on the internet, read documentation, check hardware or API specs, compare options, or investigate a technical question and want only a distilled, cited answer back — not raw search results in your own context. Fan out several researcher subagents in parallel for breadth (one per sub-question, source, or perspective); each returns a short structured report. Read-only: it never edits or writes project files. ([steven112163/dotharness](https://github.com/steven112163/dotharness/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use for questions that need sources outside the repository - official docs of a framework, library or API, version and deprecation facts, comparing libraries or approaches. Returns cited findings. Never edits files. Not for searching the current codebase (use scout). ([sue-btis/ChangeSmith](https://github.com/sue-btis/ChangeSmith/blob/HEAD/compact-change-skills/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Static DS2/BW research - Tank/GAS/ASP/PRS/save formats, Skrit semantics and call classification, reproducible analysis scripts. ([sunbrolynk/Re-Sieged](https://github.com/sunbrolynk/Re-Sieged/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: 특정 거장(인물)에 대한 1차 자료를 깊이 수집하는 에이전트. 책/논문/강연 transcript/인터뷰에서 직접 인용·출처·맥락을 raw 상태로 모은다. 요약·해석은 절대 하지 않는다. "X 자료조사", "X에 대해 조사해줘", "research X", "X에 대한 1차 자료" 같은 요청에 사용한다. ([sunwon12/mimesis](https://github.com/sunwon12/mimesis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigates unknowns before work begins. Explores the codebase, reads ADRs and CONTEXT.md, surfaces relevant prior decisions, and summarises findings for the Engineer or a specialist agent. Runs before any significant feature work to ensure the implementing agent has accurate situational awareness. Prevents the Engineer from re-discovering known patterns or violating existing decisions made in prior sessions. ([surya-manne/velocity](https://github.com/surya-manne/velocity/blob/HEAD/core/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research on one question: codebase investigation, external best practice, library or API behaviour, comparing options. Ends with one recommendation, not a menu. ([sydneydesign/Orchestrator](https://github.com/sydneydesign/Orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research specialist that searches the web for information, synthesizes findings, and stores key facts in memory for future reference. ([syesildag/aiagent](https://github.com/syesildag/aiagent/blob/HEAD/.aiagent/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research agent. Finds information either INSIDE the project (code, config, docs, git history) OR on the INTERNET, and returns a strictly structured report. Use when the user asks to "find", "look up", "research", "investigate", "where is", "does the codebase have", or to gather external facts/best-practices. The agent never writes or edits files, never runs a deep-research harness, and honestly reports what it could NOT find. If the request is ambiguous or contains no concrete question, it asks clarifying questions first (interview mode) instead of guessing. - **researcher**: Source-cited web researcher for one bounded angle of a topic - the worker the deep-research skill spawns in parallel, also usable for its validate pass. - **researcher-opus**: Deep codebase researcher using Opus for complex reasoning. Use for architecture analysis, cross-system data flow tracing, or when Sonnet's research output lacks depth. ([arndvs/ctrlshft](https://github.com/arndvs/ctrlshft/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher-opus.md))
- **researcher-opus**: Workflow-only researcher for full-research/search-paper-core; never invoke. ([beCyborg/jadlis-research](https://github.com/beCyborg/jadlis-research/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher-opus.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher-opus**: Read-only web research worker on Opus 5.5. Medium effort by default, for fact-gathering whose answer shape is known in advance — lookups, figures, versions, pricing, enumerating options; the lead sets the Agent call's `effort` to high for decision-grade synthesis — conflicting or contested sources, credibility judgment, findings that would change a plan. Preloads frontier-search; the brief sets its search budget and return shape. Reports cited findings; never edits, never changes git state. ([BigCactusLabs/agent-skills](https://github.com/BigCactusLabs/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/orchestrator/agents/researcher-opus.md))
- **researcher-opus**: Deep read-only investigation across a codebase, docs, papers or the web when the answer decides an architecture, a scientific claim, or a multi-file design. Returns a sourced conclusion, not file dumps. Use for hard questions with ambiguity or many moving parts; for a quick lookup use researcher-sonnet. ([idossha/.dotfiles](https://github.com/idossha/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/researcher-opus.md))
- **researcher-opus48**: Workflow-only fallback after a safety-classifier stop: full-research-core re-runs a stopped channel, curator, verifier or escalation role here once, on Opus 4.8 (the documented cyber-fallback model); never invoke manually. ([beCyborg/jadlis-research](https://github.com/beCyborg/jadlis-research/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher-opus48.md))

([TheBitcoinBreakdown-95/claude-research-agent](https://github.com/TheBitcoinBreakdown-95/claude-research-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/RESEARCHER-ORCHESTRATION.md))

- **researchos-analyst**: Use for open-ended research questions over ResearchOS data — "what's the state of project X", "who worked on Y and what did they contribute", "has anything like this been tried before across our projects", multi-step lineage/credit/negative-results investigations that need several MCP calls chained together. Also handles ad hoc write requests the eight fixed-shape skills don't cover — e.g. "log this experiment and then check whether anything like it failed before" — by chaining a write tool in after read tools, always confirming first. Never writes without explicit confirmation. ([Ayushkiller/researchos-plugins](https://github.com/Ayushkiller/researchos-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/researchos/agents/researchos-analyst.md))
- **researchos-analyst**: Analyst Agent synthesizes literature into research interpretation, hypotheses, and post-experiment analysis. Owns Stage 3 (research map), Stage 4 (gap proposal), Stage 6 (hypothesis brainstorm with rivals), and Stage 8 (result analysis with statistics). Does NOT decide hypothesis final status or modify raw results. ([Fisherman27/ResearchOS](https://github.com/Fisherman27/ResearchOS/blob/HEAD/AgentsForWorkbuddy/agents/researchos-analyst.md))
- **researchos-critic**: Critic Agent independently executes the mandatory 3-layer falsification gate (Layer 1: 10-item checklist with five-section records, Layer 2: Devil's Advocate with limitation/critical-alternative classification, Layer 3: Claim-Evidence alignment) and outputs three explicit fields (experiment_valid / result_direction / conclusion_scope). Uses a separate execution instance from the Experiment/Analyst under review. Cannot modify raw results or write hypothesis final status. ([Fisherman27/ResearchOS](https://github.com/Fisherman27/ResearchOS/blob/HEAD/AgentsForWorkbuddy/agents/researchos-critic.md))
- **researchos-experiment**: Experiment Agent designs, implements, self-checks, and runs experiments that directly test a confirmed Hypothesis against a fair baseline. Owns Stage 7 (design with comparison protocol, mechanism probe gate, minimal raw output contract, run). Does NOT do formal scientific analysis or modify the Hypothesis. ([Fisherman27/ResearchOS](https://github.com/Fisherman27/ResearchOS/blob/HEAD/AgentsForWorkbuddy/agents/researchos-experiment.md))
- **researchos-literature**: Literature Agent executes the full Stage 2 literature protocol (dual-path search, PDF structure extraction, graded reading, anchor-verified Claims/Evidence, Related Work backfill, per-round 8-20 budget), Stage 4 novelty searches, and citation/source fixes raised by Critic. Delivers traceable, boundary-clear literature materials. ([Fisherman27/ResearchOS](https://github.com/Fisherman27/ResearchOS/blob/HEAD/AgentsForWorkbuddy/agents/researchos-literature.md))
- **researchos-team-lead**: Research Team Lead orchestrates the 11-stage ResearchOS loop, dispatches tasks to Literature/Analyst/Experiment/Critic members, verifies deliverables, maintains persistent research state, and produces evidence-backed Decisions. Owns Stage 1 (question), Stage 5 (gap review), Stage 10 (decision matrix), Stage 11 (round-end), and the single serialized commit entry for all research artifacts. ([Fisherman27/ResearchOS](https://github.com/Fisherman27/ResearchOS/blob/HEAD/AgentsForWorkbuddy/agents/researchos-team-lead.md))
- **researchos-team-lead**: Lead orchestrator for the ResearchOS WorkBuddy expert team. Creates one native team, keeps stable member sessions, routes engine work orders, resumes runs, and reports audited research outputs to the user. ([Fisherman27/ResearchOSForWorkBuddy](https://github.com/Fisherman27/ResearchOSForWorkBuddy/blob/HEAD/agents/researchos-team-lead.md))

- **ResearchSynthesizer**: Synthesizes supplied research material — internal docs, code and supplied web sources — into a cited brief. - **resection-implementation-reader**: Cold reader of a project's actual current implementation — source, tests, config, and observable running behavior via read-only commands. Given a project root and a fixed list of claims (not its own to add to or remove from), scores each by inspecting the real code, never the project's own docs about itself. - **resection-vision-reader**: Closed-book cold reader of a project's vision/goal material. Receives the project's vision documents inlined verbatim in its prompt — carries only TaskStop, a tool with no file or network access, kept non-empty because Claude Code silently grants the full default tool set to an agent definition with an empty tools list. Cannot read source, tests, or anything not handed to it. Two modes selected by the orchestrator's instruction: (1) enumerate claims, (2) score a given claim list. Never both in a way that lets it see its own prior verdicts. ([justinstimatze/resection](https://github.com/justinstimatze/resection/blob/HEAD/agents/resection-vision-reader.md) · ★1)

- **resident**: General-purpose subagent with every tool that stays alive for a whole run, works in a loop and reports each finding to its caller with SendMessage, ending its turn only on the stop condition its brief names. Use it instead of general-purpose when a background subagent has to watch something or keep working and message you as it goes, rather than end its turn with one report. The brief names what to watch or do, what to report, the heartbeat interval and the stop condition. - **resident**: Long-lived reviewer, expensive (1 hour cache, a cache write costs 1.6 times more than the 5-minute one). Use it only when one big picture of the project serves several tasks with pauses of 5 to 60 minutes, and keep going with it through SendMessage. After an hour start a new one. Otherwise use reviewer. - **resident-my-home**: The resident sidebar labels the existing `/resident/move-in/*` section **My home**.
